- **spatial-data-architect**: 空間與地理資料分析架構師(區域研究 / hedonic 房價 / 不動產實證)。把帶有地址或經緯度的資料變成可分析、可畫圖、可發表的空間研究資料集。核心能力:(1)LLM 輔助地理編碼＋驗證退路——地址轉座標後,用行政區界/中心點/白名單比對抓出離群與幻覺,絕不讓 AI 亂猜的座標進資料;(2)H3 六角網格空間聚合——面積相等、鄰居一致、解析度可調,比行政區更適合密度與比較,且天然去識別;(3)座標系統紀律——台灣官方 TWD97(EPSG:3826,公尺)與 WGS84(EPSG:4326,經緯度)不可混用,附轉換與誤用防線;(4)空間計量意識——空間自相關(Moran's I)、空間落後/誤差模型的診斷,估計交棒 r-spss;(5)出版級空間地圖——區域面量圖 choropleth、六角密度圖 hexbin 的出圖配方(靜態、可放論文與簡報),與 management-figure 的統計圖劃分。附已實測的 H3 聚合腳本。何時用:全台不動產/房價的區域分析、把成交點聚合成網格、地址轉座標要驗證、公司地理定位、要畫區域地圖、hedonic 定價模型的空間資料準備、鄰近效果/空間外溢。觸發詞:空間分析、地理資料、GIS、地理編碼、geocoding、經緯度、座標轉換、TWD97、WGS84、EPSG、H3、六角網格、hexbin、choropleth、面量圖、區域地圖、空間… ([Nero1688/claude-academic-skills](https://github.com/Nero1688/claude-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-data-architect) · ★13 · 3 copies)
- **spatial-data-engineer**: ETL specialist who transforms messy geospatial data from any source into clean, standardized, production-ready datasets — format conversion, CRS reprojection, attribute normalization, and automated pipelines. ([amirhamzakhan2001/fehm](https://github.com/amirhamzakhan2001/fehm/tree/HEAD/skills/domain/gis/spatial-data-engineer))
- **spatial-data-integration**: 複数の地理データを、分析の単位として決めた単位に結合する。点を面に入れる、最近傍に割り当てる、キーで結ぶ、単位ごとに集計する。結合の前後で全ての行を数え、落ちた行と重複した行を報告する。 ([yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills](https://github.com/yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/spatial-data-integration) · ★3)
- **spatial-data-integration-preprocessing**: Use when when you have paired spatial transcriptome and metabolome datasets (in .h5ad or matrix format) with spatial location information, and you need to establish correspondence between features across modalities before performing spatial morphological alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spatial-data-integration-preprocessing))
- **spatial-data-io**: --&gt; ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-transcriptomics-analysis/bioSkills/spatial-data-io) · ★3.1k · 13 copies)
- **spatial-data-management**: Inspect, validate, copy, repair, reproject and manage fields in vector and raster spatial datasets, and calculate grouped attribute statistics. Use for GeoJSON, GeoPackage, Shapefile, GeoTIFF, COG and other GeoPandas/Rasterio-readable files when an agent needs dataset metadata, CRS checks, schema changes or table summaries. ([lihao77/RAGSystem](https://github.com/lihao77/RAGSystem/tree/HEAD/plugins/backend-plugin-skills/skills/spatial-data-management) · ★4)
- **spatial-data-mocking**: Playbook per il testing offline del frontend Angular 21 in totale isolamento dal backend Python. Definisce il set di dati mockati, le istruzioni per attivare/disattivare il mock service, e i checklist visivi per validare cluster, overlay full-bleed, hatching SVG e carousel PrimeNG senza necessità del backend attivo o di dati reali da Miniflux/Gemini. **Sidebar freeze:** validare il carosello in sola osservazione — non modificare `radar-sidebar/**` né sostituire `p-carousel` con `article-list`. ([JiraaG/Intelligence-Dashboard](https://github.com/JiraaG/Intelligence-Dashboard/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/spatial-data-mocking) · ★1)
- **spatial-data-modeling**: 基準 (平均、グループ平均、線形) と同じ分割、同じ指標で、線形、木、ブースティングなどのモデルを当てはめて比べる。回帰と分類の両方。モデルの種類ごとの前処理と落とし穴は references にある。 ([yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills](https://github.com/yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/spatial-data-modeling) · ★3)
- **spatial-data-qa**: Quality-checking spatial data before it is used or delivered, covering invalid geometry (self-intersections, ring orientation), null and empty shapes, duplicates, mixed geometry types, slivers, gaps and overlaps, topology, schema and domain checks, and CRS sanity, and what to fix, flag or send to the data owner. Use it when asked to "QA this layer", "validate geometry", "check for duplicates", "fix invalid polygons", "is this data clean", or before handing any result to someone else. ([wiatrek-kevin-s/columbus](https://github.com/wiatrek-kevin-s/columbus/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-data-qa) · ★2)
- **spatial-data-quality**: Keep spatial data trustworthy: geometry validity (ST_IsValid / ST_MakeValid, self-intersections, ring order), topology, declared-vs-actual SRID checks, coordinate-order (lon/lat) sanity, and validating geometry at load time so bad data never lands. Reach for this when loading a dataset, debugging a function that crashes on some rows, or hardening an ingest. Used by `geospatial-data-engineer` (primary). ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/tree/HEAD/plugins/geospatial-engineering/skills/spatial-data-quality) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **spatial-data-scientist**: Advanced spatial analytics specialist who applies statistical modeling, spatial econometrics, clustering, and predictive analytics to geospatial data — finding patterns that aren't visible on a map. ([amirhamzakhan2001/fehm](https://github.com/amirhamzakhan2001/fehm/tree/HEAD/skills/domain/gis/spatial-data-scientist))
- **spatial-data-validation**: Validate and inspect spatial transcriptomics data files (SpatialData/zarr/AnnData) before any analysis. Use when the user points at a spatial data file, reports a loading/format error, or asks whether data is suitable for a method. ([Rebell-Leader/SpatialAI_MCP](https://github.com/Rebell-Leader/SpatialAI_MCP/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/spatial-data-validation) · ★4 · 4 copies)
